\displaystyle+6\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}\}+\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{l\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{l\cdot})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Big\{48\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{4}_{i})+32\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{3}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+12\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+12\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{4}(\mathbf{M}_{i})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}f_{\gamma_{2}}+\gamma_{4}f_{\gamma_{4}}+\gamma_{6}f_{\gamma_{6}}+\gamma_{1}^{2}f_{\gamma_{1}^{2}}+\gamma_{2}^{2}f_{\gamma_{2}^{2}}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}f_{\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+3\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big(\left[2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\right]

\displaystyle\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\times\left[2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j})+\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{j})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\right]\Big)

\displaystyle+4\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big(\gamma_{4}\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+12\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+6\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{T})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)+6\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle+8\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\Big)\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})

\displaystyle+6\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}[2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}]\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{k})

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{k})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{l}),

where
f_{\gamma_{2}},\,f_{\gamma_{4}},\,f_{\gamma_{6}},\,f_{\gamma_{1}^{2}},\,f_{\gamma_{2}^{2}},\,f_{\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}}
are the same as above,
and

\displaystyle\Big\{\mathbb{E}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Big[4\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{4}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+4\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\gamma_{2}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big[4\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j})+2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{j})+2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})+\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{j})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\{(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\Big]

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big[2\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{j})+8\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})+4\mathrm{tr}^{3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\Big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big[4\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{k})+4\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{k})+2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{k})

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{k}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{k}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}\Big]

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\left\{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{k})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{l})\right\}.

So, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\bigg\}=O(NT^{3}+N^{2}T^{3}+N^{3}T^{3}).

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(b^{2}_{N})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{4}}\Bigg[2\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\bigg\}+\frac{2}{T^{2}}\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{1}{NT}\right).

\Box

S.2.3
Proof of Lemma
S.1.8

Recall that
\mathbf{E}=\mathbf{U}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2},

then we have

\hat{\epsilon}_{li}\hat{\epsilon}_{lj}={Z}^{\prime}\left[\{u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\otimes\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}\}\right]{Z},

where

Z=(z_{11},\cdots,z_{1T},z_{21},\cdots,z_{2T},\cdots,z_{N1},\cdots,z_{NT})^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{NT},

u_{l}^{1/2}\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
denotes the
l
-th row vector of matrix
\mathbf{U}^{1/2},
and
m_{i}^{l}\in\mathbb{R}^{T}
represents the
i
-th row vector of matrix
\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}.

Hence,
\sum_{l=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{li}\hat{\epsilon}_{lj}={Z}^{\prime}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{N}\{u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\otimes\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}\}\right]{Z}.

To simplify notation, we write
\mathbf{W}_{ij}
as matrix
\sum_{l=1}^{N}\{u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\otimes\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}\}.

Recall that
||\mathbf{A}||
denotes the operator norm of matrix
\mathbf{A}.

So, by the Hanson-Wright inequality in
Rudelson and Vershynin 2013
,
we can obtain that for every
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}\left\{\left|{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{ij}{Z}-\mathbb{E}\big({Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{ij}{Z}\big)\right|>t\right\}\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{K^{4}\|\mathbf{W}_{ij}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}},\frac{t}{K^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{ij}\|}\right)\right].

Obviously,

\mathbb{E}\big({Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{ij}{Z}\big)=\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{W}_{ij})=\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}\sigma_{ij,l},
where
u_{ll}=(\mathbf{U})_{ll},
for
1\leq l\leq N.

Recall that we assume that
u_{ll}=1,

for
1\leq l\leq N.

According to the properties of the Kronecker product,
we have

\mathrm{tr}\{\mathbf{W}_{ij}(\mathbf{W}_{ij})^{\prime}\}=\underset{l,s=1}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}^{2}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{ii}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{jj}.

Due to
(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{jj}=\sigma_{jj}-(\mathbf{B}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}-(\mathbf{B}_{s}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}-(\mathbf{B}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{B}_{s})_{jj}
and (
S.2.1
),
we have
\mathrm{tr}\{\mathbf{W}_{ij}(\mathbf{W}_{ij})^{\prime}\}=\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}\{1+o(1)\}

and
\mathrm{tr}\{\mathbf{W}_{ij}\mathbf{W}_{ij}\}=\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\sigma_{ij}^{2}\{1+o(1)\}.
Then, we focus on
\|\mathbf{W}_{ij}\|.

According to the triangle inequality of norms, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{ij}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\|+\|\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{max}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}}+\|\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\|,

where
\sigma_{i}^{1/2}\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
denotes the
i
-th row vector of matrix
\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}.

Then, using the definition of spectral norm, we can obtain that for any vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{NT}
with norm
\|x\|=1,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\|x\|=1}x^{\prime}\Big[\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{sl}u_{s}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{s}(m_{i}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]x

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\|x\|=1}x^{\prime}\Big[\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]x

\displaystyle+\max_{\|x\|=1}x^{\prime}\Big[\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{s}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{s}(m_{j}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}.

Next, we first calculate
\Delta_{1},

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}=

\displaystyle\max_{\|x\|=1}x^{\prime}\Big[\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]x

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Big[\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{N}\mathrm{tr}\Big[u_{ll}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{l}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\Big[\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{l}(m_{j}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}^{2}\Big\{\sigma_{ii,l}\sigma_{jj,l}-2(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{ii}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}+\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(T^{0\vee(1-\frac{1}{\tau})})

where the last inequality holds due to (
S.2.1
). Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}=

\displaystyle\max_{\|x\|=1}x^{\prime}\Big[\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{s}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{s}(m_{j}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]x

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Big[\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{s}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{s}(m_{j}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathrm{tr}\Big[u_{ls}u_{l}^{1/2}(u_{s}^{1/2})^{\prime}\otimes\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{s}(m_{j}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\Big[\big\{m_{j}^{l}(m_{i}^{l})^{\prime}-\sigma_{j}^{1/2}(\sigma_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\big\{m_{i}^{s}(m_{j}^{s})^{\prime}-\sigma_{i}^{1/2}(\sigma_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}^{2}\Big\{(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{ii}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{jj}-(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{ii}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}-(\mathbf{P}_{s}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}(\mathbf{P}_{s}\mathbf{\Sigma})_{jj}+\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(T^{0\vee(1-\frac{1}{\tau})}).

So, we can conclude that
\|\mathbf{W}_{ij}\|=O(T^{0\vee(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\tau})}).

Then, according to Theorem 1.1 in
Rudelson and Vershynin 2013
,
for every
t\in(0,o(N^{\frac{1}{2}\wedge\frac{1}{2\tau}})),

there exist a constant
c>0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{l=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{li}\hat{\epsilon}_{lj}-\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}\sigma_{ij,l}\right|>t\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})(\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}+\sigma_{ij}^{2})}\right)

(S.2.6)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\Big(-c\frac{t^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})(\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}+\sigma_{ij}^{2})}{K^{4}\mathrm{tr}\{\mathbf{W}_{ij}(\mathbf{W}_{ij})^{\prime}\}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\Big(-c^{\prime}\frac{t^{2}}{K^{4}}\Big)

where
\mathrm{tr}\{\mathbf{W}_{ij}(\mathbf{W}_{ij})^{\prime}\}=\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\sigma_{ii}\sigma_{jj}\{1+o(1)\},

and
c^{\prime}>0
is a positive constant independent of
N
and
T.

In fact,
\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}=\sum_{l=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{li}/N
can be seen as a linear combination of
z_{ks},

1\leq k,s\leq N,
where
z_{ks}
is the element at row
k
and column
s
of random matrix
\mathbf{Z}.
Then, by classical Cramér type large deviation results for independent random variables(see Corollary 3.1 in
Saulis and Statulevicius 1991
), we have for any
\varepsilon>0,

\mathbb{P}\left(|\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}|>x\sqrt{\mathrm{var}(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j})}\right)\leq C\exp\left\{-\frac{x^{2}}{2}(1-\varepsilon)\right\},

uniformly in
x\in[0,o(\sqrt{N})).

For
\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j},
we have

\mathrm{var}(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j})=\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ll}\sigma_{ii,l}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\underset{l\neq s}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{ls}(\mathbf{P}_{l}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{s})_{ii}=O(N^{-1\vee-\frac{1}{\tau}}),

uniformly in
1\leq j\leq N.

So, for any
\varepsilon>0,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot i}\right|\geq x^{2}\sqrt{\mathrm{var}\left(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}\right)\mathrm{var}\left(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot i}\right)}\right)\leq 2C\exp\left\{-\frac{x^{2}}{2}(1-\varepsilon)\right\}

uniformly in
x\in[0,o(\sqrt{N}))
. We have, uniformly for
x\in[0,o(N^{\frac{1}{2}\wedge\left(\frac{1}{\tau}-\frac{1}{2}\right)})),

x^{2}\sqrt{\mathrm{var}\left(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot i}\right)\mathrm{var}\left(\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}\right)}=

o(x/\sqrt{N})
. So for any

\varepsilon>0,
large
N
, and any
\delta>0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot i}\bar{\hat{\epsilon}}_{\cdot j}\right|\geq\delta\frac{x}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\leq 2C\exp\left\{-\frac{x^{2}}{2}(1-\varepsilon)\right\},

(S.2.7)

uniformly and
x\in[0,o(N^{\frac{1}{2}\wedge\left(\frac{1}{\tau}-\frac{1}{2}\right)})).

Then, the lemma follows from (
S.2.6
) and (
S.2.7
).
\Box

S.2.4
Proof of Lemma
S.1.9

Recall that without loss of generality, we assume that
u_{ll}=1,

for
1\leq l\leq N.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{2}

\displaystyle\{\mathrm{tr}(\hat{\mathbf{\Gamma}})\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle T^{2}a_{N}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\gamma_{N}}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle T^{2}b_{N}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\left[\sum_{i=1}\sum_{j=1}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\left(\sum_{i=1}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i=1}\sum_{j=1}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\right]

it is easy to verify that
a_{N}
and
b_{N}
will make the equation (
S.1.12
) true.

In the following, we prove that
a_{N},b_{N}
satisfy the properties in the lemma.
We first deal with the term
a_{N}.

Recall that
Z=(z_{11},\cdots,z_{1T},z_{21},\cdots,z_{2T},\cdots,z_{N1},\cdots,z_{NT})^{\prime}

and
u_{l}^{1/2}\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
denotes the
l
-th row vector of matrix
\mathbf{U}^{1/2},

we have

(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})={Z}^{\prime}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\otimes(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right]Z.

Therefore, due to Lemma
S.1.4
,

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i\neq j\leq N}u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{j}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}.

Similarly, due to
\sum_{1\leq i\neq j\leq N}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]=O(N),
we have

\displaystyle{T^{2}}\gamma_{N}a_{N}

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i\neq j\leq N}u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{j}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\Big]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma})\{1-O(T^{-1})\}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle{T^{2}}\gamma_{N}a_{N}

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i\neq j\leq N}u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\}+u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})+u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[\{u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\circ\{u_{j}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}\}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{U}^{2})\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{\Sigma})\{1+O(NT^{-1})\}.

This proves that
a_{N}
satisfies the inequality in the lemma.
It remains to calculate
b_{N}.
We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\right\}^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}^{2}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\right\}.

First,

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left[\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{4}\right\}-\mathbb{E}^{2}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\}+2\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{4}\right\}-\mathbb{E}^{2}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+4\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+2\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot})^{2}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot})^{2}\}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+4\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot})^{2}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{l\cdot})^{2}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\}\mathbb{E}\{(\hat{\epsilon}_{k\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{l\cdot})^{2}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle G_{1}+G_{2}+G_{3}+G_{4}+G_{5}+G_{6}+G_{7}.

Recall that
\mathbf{Q}_{ij}=\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}.
Let
\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\doteq u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}
for
1\leq i,j\leq N.

First, we focus on
G_{1}.

According to Lemma
S.1.4
, we have

G_{1}=G_{1_{1}}+\gamma_{2}G_{1_{2}}+\gamma_{4}G_{1_{3}}+\gamma_{6}G_{1_{4}}+\gamma_{1}^{2}G_{1_{5}}+\gamma_{2}^{2}G_{1_{6}}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}G_{1_{7}},

where

\displaystyle G_{1_{1}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}8u_{ii}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)+8u_{ii}^{4}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)+32u_{ii}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}^{3}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+48u_{ii}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{4}\right),

\displaystyle G_{1_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+8u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+48u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)u_{ii}^{2}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{3}\right\}

\displaystyle+48\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\right\}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\right\}\tau_{T},

\displaystyle G_{1_{3}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+24\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}),

\displaystyle G_{1_{4}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right),

\displaystyle G_{1_{5}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left[\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\right]\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left[\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\}\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\}\right]\tau_{T}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})

\displaystyle+48\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\right\}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\right\}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+16\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+96\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}\prime\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\},

\displaystyle G_{1_{6}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+8\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T},

\displaystyle G_{1_{7}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+32\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}.

Obviously,
G_{1_{1}}=O(NT^{3}).

Due to Lemma
S.1.3
(1),
we have

\displaystyle G_{1_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+8u_{ii}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+48u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)u_{ii}^{2}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{3}\right\}

\displaystyle+48\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\right\}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\right\}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{3})+O(NT^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{N}48u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)u_{ii}^{2}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{3}\right\}

\displaystyle+48\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\right\}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2}\right)\right\}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{3})+O(NT^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{N}48u_{ii}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}

\displaystyle+96u_{ii}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{3})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}+48u_{ii}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(NT^{3})+O(NT^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{N}48u_{ii}^{4}|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)||\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}|

\displaystyle+96u_{ii}^{4}|\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{3})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}|+48u_{ii}^{4}|\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}^{2}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{3})+O(NT^{2})+O(NT).

Then,

\displaystyle G_{1_{3}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+24\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}^{3}+24u_{ii}\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}4u_{ii}^{4}|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)||\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}^{3}|+24u_{ii}^{4}|\sum_{s=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ss}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{2})+O(NT).

Similarly, we can easily obtain that
G_{1_{4}}=O(NT).

Note that for any square matrix
\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{T\times T},

|\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{A})_{st}|\leq\sqrt{T^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right)}.

So,
we next have

\displaystyle G_{1_{5}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left[\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\right]\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left[\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\}\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\}\right]\tau_{T}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})

\displaystyle+48\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\right\}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\right\}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+16\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+96\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}\prime\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{tt}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}

\displaystyle+48u_{ii}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{tt}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{st}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}

\displaystyle+16u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{3}

\displaystyle+96\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\sum_{q=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{qt}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{tt}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{sq}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\sum_{q=1}^{N}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{qt}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{sq}^{2}

\displaystyle+96u_{ii}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}^{2}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ts}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{ts}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24u_{ii}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C|(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}|+48u_{ii}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C|(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{st}|

\displaystyle+16u_{ii}^{3}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}+96u_{ii}^{3}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\sum_{q=1}^{N}|(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{qt}|(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})_{qq}

\displaystyle+96u_{ii}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{3/2})+O(NT^{2})+O(NT).

Similarly, for
G_{1_{6}},

we have

\displaystyle G_{1_{6}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)

\displaystyle+24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+8\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\big\{\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}\big\}^{2}\big\{\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}^{2}\big\}^{2}+24\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}^{2}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{tt}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}

\displaystyle+8\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}2u_{ii}^{4}\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}^{2})+24u_{ii}^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}+8u_{ii}^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{2})+O(NT).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle G_{1_{7}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle+32\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{tt}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{tt}^{2}

\displaystyle+32\sum_{s=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{st}^{3}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii})_{ss}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{3}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{ss}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}24u_{ii}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C|(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}|+32u_{ii}^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})_{st}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(NT^{3/2})+O(NT).

Combining the above calculations, we can get
G_{1}=O(NT^{3}).

We next deal with
G_{2}
. Similarly, due to Lemma
S.1.4
, we have

G_{2}=G_{2_{1}}+\gamma_{2}G_{2_{2}}+\gamma_{4}G_{2_{3}}+\gamma_{6}G_{2_{4}}+\gamma_{1}^{2}G_{2_{5}}+\gamma_{2}^{2}G_{2_{6}}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}G_{2_{7}},

where

\displaystyle G_{2_{1}}=

\displaystyle 2\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}4u_{ij}^{4}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{2}\right)+4u_{ij}^{2}u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)

\displaystyle+2u_{ij}^{4}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{2}\right)+2u_{ii}^{2}u_{jj}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)+4u_{ij}^{2}u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)

\displaystyle+24u_{ij}^{2}u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{3}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)+12u_{ij}^{2}u_{ii}u_{jj}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{2}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}^{2}\right)+6u_{ii}^{2}u_{jj}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right),

\displaystyle+3\Big[4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)^{2}\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ij})\right\}^{2}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{jj}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}^{2}\right)\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ij})\right\}\tau_{T}

\displaystyle G_{2_{3}}=

\displaystyle G_{2_{4}}=

\displaystyle 2\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right),

\displaystyle+16u_{ij}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\frac{1}{4}\Big[\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+2\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\right\}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle G_{2_{6}}=

\displaystyle 2\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big\{\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

Because

Because

and Lemma

,

. We next deal with

.
Similarly,

G_{4}=G_{4_{1}}+\gamma_{2}G_{4_{2}}+\gamma_{4}G_{4_{3}}+\gamma_{6}G_{4_{4}}+\gamma_{1}^{2}G_{4_{5}}+\gamma_{2}^{2}G_{4_{6}}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}G_{4_{7}},

where

Because
\underset{i\neq j}{\sum\sum}u_{ij}^{4}<\underset{i\neq j}{\sum\sum}u_{ij}^{2}<\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{U}^{2}\right)=O(N),

we have

Similarly,

\underset{i\neq j}{\sum\sum}|u_{ij}|=O(N^{1\vee(2-1/\tau)})

and Lemma
S.1.2
,
we have
G_{4_{2}}=O(N^{1\vee(2-1/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{2}T^{2}),

G_{4_{5}}=O(N^{2}T^{5/2}),

G_{4_{6}}=O(N^{2}T^{2})

and
G_{4_{7}}=O(N^{2}T^{2}).

Combining the above calculations, we can get
G_{4}=O(N^{2}T^{5/2}+N^{1\vee(2-1/\tau)}T^{3})
. We next deal with
G_{5}

Similarly, due to Lemma
S.1.4

Because
\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum\sum\sum}|u_{ij}u_{jk}|=O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)})

and

\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum\sum\sum}u_{ij}^{2}=O(N^{2}),

we have
G_{5_{1}}=O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{2}T^{2}).

Similarly,
due to
\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum\sum}|u_{ij}|=O(N^{2\vee(3-1/\tau)}),

and Lemma
S.1.2
,
we have
G_{5_{2}}=O(N^{2\vee(3-1/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{3}T^{2}),

G_{5_{3}}=O(N^{2\vee(3-1/\tau)}T^{2})+O(N^{3}T),

G_{5_{4}}=O(N^{3}T),

G_{5_{5}}=O(N^{3}T^{5/2}),

G_{5_{6}}=O(N^{3}T^{2})

and
G_{5_{7}}=O(N^{3}T^{2}).

Combining the above calculations, we can get
G_{5}=O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{3}T^{5/2})
. We next deal with
G_{6}
.
Similarly, due to Lemma
S.1.4

Due to

we have
G_{6_{1}}=O(N^{2}T^{3}+N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)}T^{3}).

Similarly,
due to
\underset{i\neq j\neq k}{\sum\sum}|u_{ij}|=O(N^{2\vee(3-1/\tau)})

S.1.2
,
we have

G_{6_{6}}=O(N^{3}T^{2})

and
G_{6_{7}}=O(N^{3}T^{2}).

Combining the above calculations, we can get
G_{6}=O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{3}T^{2}+O(N^{2\vee(3-1/\tau)}T^{5/2})
