\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)u_{kl}\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)u_{kl}\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)u_{ll}\mathbf{\Omega}_{kk}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)u_{kk}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ll}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)u_{kl}\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)u_{jj}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{jj}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{jj}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{jj}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{jj}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ii}\mathbf{\Omega}_{jj}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)u_{ij}\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right\}\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right\}\big],

\displaystyle G_{7_{6}}=

\displaystyle\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)

\displaystyle+4\big\{\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}\big\}

\displaystyle+\big\{\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}\big\},

\displaystyle G_{7_{7}}=

\displaystyle\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2\big\{\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}\big\}

\displaystyle+\big\{2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\tau_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{N}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{N}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{ji}\right)\tau_{N}\tau_{T}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)\left(\mathbf{Q}_{lk}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ji}\right)\tau_{T}\big\}.

Due to

\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}u_{kj}^{2}u_{il}^{2}=O(N^{2}),

\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|u_{ij}u_{jl}u_{kk}u_{li}|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|u_{ij}|\sum_{l=1}^{N}|u_{li}|=O(N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)})

and

\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{kl}u_{li}|\leq\underset{i\neq j}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|u_{ij}|\underset{k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|u_{kl}|=O(N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)}),

we have
G_{7_{1}}=O(N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)}T^{3}).

Similarly,
due to

\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|u_{ij}|=O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}),

and Lemma
S.1.2
,
we have
G_{7_{2}}=O(N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{2}),

G_{7_{3}}=O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{2})+O(N^{4}T),

G_{7_{4}}=O(N^{4}T),

G_{7_{5}}=O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{5/2})+O(N^{4}T^{3/2}),

and
G_{7_{7}}=O(N^{4}T^{3/2}).

Here, we focus on calculating the first term of
G_{7_{6}}.

Because

\displaystyle\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}2\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{kl}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(T^{2})\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}\circ\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(T^{2})\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij})_{ss}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl})_{ss}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(T^{2})\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\sum_{s=1}^{N}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij})_{ss}^{2}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl})_{ss}^{2}

\displaystyle+O(T^{2})\underset{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\underset{s_{1}\neq s_{2}}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}|(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij})_{s_{1}s_{1}}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl})_{s_{1}s_{1}}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij})_{s_{2}s_{2}}(\mathbf{\Omega}_{kl})_{s_{2}s_{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(NT^{2}+N^{2}T^{2}),

where the last inequality holds due to
\mathbf{\Omega}_{ij}=u_{i}^{1/2}(u_{j}^{1/2})^{\prime}

and
(u_{i}^{1/2})^{\prime}u_{i}^{1/2}=1,
for all
1\leq i,j\leq N.

Hence, we have
G_{7_{6}}=O(N^{2}T^{2})+O(N^{4}T^{3/2}).

and
G_{7}=O(N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)}T^{3})+O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{2})+O(N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{5/2})+O(N^{4}T^{3/2})
.
Combining
G_{1},\cdots,G_{7},

we can conclude that

\mathrm{var}\Big[\big\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\big\}^{2}\Big]=O(N^{4}T^{3/2}+N^{2}T^{3}+N^{3}T^{5/2}+N^{2\vee(4-2/\tau)}T^{3}+N^{3\vee(4-1/\tau)}T^{5/2}).

Similarly, for

\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}Z^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\otimes\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)Z=Z^{\prime}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\Omega}_{ii}\otimes\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\right)\right\}Z\doteq Z^{\prime}\mathbf{J}Z.

So, we have

\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\bigg\}=\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(Z^{\prime}\mathbf{J}Z\Big)^{2}\bigg\}.

Again using Lemma
S.1.4
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(Z^{\prime}\mathbf{J}Z\Big)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big(Z^{\prime}\mathbf{J}Z\Big)^{4}-\mathbb{E}^{2}\Big(Z^{\prime}\mathbf{J}Z\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle K_{1}+\gamma_{2}K_{2}+\gamma_{4}K_{3}+\gamma_{6}K_{4}+\gamma_{1}^{2}K_{5}+\gamma_{2}^{2}K_{6}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}K_{7},

where

\displaystyle K_{1}=

\displaystyle 8\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{2}\right)+8\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{J}^{2}\right)+32\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{3}\right)+48\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{4}\right),

\displaystyle K_{2}=

\displaystyle 4\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)+8{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)+48\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}^{3}\right\}+48{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}^{2}\right){\tau}_{NT},

\displaystyle K_{3}=

\displaystyle 4\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)+24\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle K_{4}=

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right),

\displaystyle K_{5}=

\displaystyle 24{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)+48{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}^{2}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\tau_{NT}

\displaystyle+16{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)+96{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\tau_{NT}

\displaystyle+96\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}\right),

\displaystyle K_{6}=

\displaystyle 3\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)+24{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}

\displaystyle+8{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT},

\displaystyle K_{7}=

\displaystyle 24{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\mathbf{J}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}+32{\tau}_{NT}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{I}_{NT}\circ\mathbf{J}\right)\left(\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\circ\mathbf{J}\right){\tau}_{NT}.

First, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}\right)=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}u_{ii}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii})=O(NT),

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{2}\right)=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}u_{ij}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\mathbf{Q}_{jj})=O(NT).

Then, due to Assumption
3
and

\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}|u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{ik}|=O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)}),

we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{3}\right)=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{ik}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\mathbf{Q}_{kk})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{U}^{3}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{3}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{ik}\left\{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\mathbf{Q}_{kk})-\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{3}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{U}^{3}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{3}\right)+O(N^{1\vee(3-2/\tau)})=O(NT).

Next, due to Assumption
3
and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}|u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{kl}u_{li}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}|u_{ij}u_{jk}|\sum_{l=1}^{N}|u_{kl}u_{li}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}u_{ij}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}u_{jk}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}C=O(N^{2}),

we can get

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{4}\right)=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{kl}u_{li}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\mathbf{Q}_{kk}\mathbf{Q}_{ll})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{U}^{4}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{4}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}u_{ij}u_{jk}u_{kl}u_{li}\left\{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\mathbf{Q}_{kk}\mathbf{Q}_{ll})-\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{4}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{U}^{4}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{4}\right)+O(N^{2})=O(NT).

Similarly, according to the above formulas, we have

K_{1}=O(N^{3}T^{3})=K_{2},

K_{3}=O(N^{2}T^{2}),

K_{4}=O(NT),

K_{5}=O(N^{3}T^{3}),

K_{6}=O(N^{2}T^{2})=K_{7}.

Then, we can conclude that

\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\bigg\}=O(N^{3}T^{3}).

Finally, by Assumption
3
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(b^{2}_{N})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{4}}\Bigg[2\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}\bigg\}+\frac{2}{T^{2}}\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{1}{T^{\frac{1}{2}\vee\left(\frac{1}{\tau}-\frac{1}{2}\right)}}\right).

\Box

S.2.5
Proof of Lemma
S.1.10

Recall that
\mathbf{M}_{i}=\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{P}_{i}.

Let

\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}=\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}=\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}z_{i},

where
z_{i}=(z_{i1},\cdots,z_{iT})^{\prime}
is the
i
-th row vector of
\mathbf{Z}.
Define
\mathbf{Q}_{ij}\doteq\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2},
then, we have
\mathbf{Q}_{ii}=\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}.

Obviously, by Assumption
1
, we have
\mathbb{E}\left(S_{N}\right)=0.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(S^{2}_{N}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\underset{i<j}{\sum\sum}\underset{s<t}{\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}}{\left\|\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}\right\|\cdot\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|}\frac{\epsilon_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{s}\mathbf{P}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}{\left\|\mathbf{P}_{s}\epsilon_{s}\right\|\cdot\left\|\mathbf{P}_{t}\epsilon_{t}\right\|}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\underset{i<j}{\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\left\|\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}\right\|^{2}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\underset{i<j}{\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}}{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|^{2}}+\frac{(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot})\cdot\{2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii})\}}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\,\,\,\,-\frac{[2(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\}]}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{i})\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|^{2}}+O(T^{-2})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\underset{i<j}{\sum\sum}\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+O(T^{-2})

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\left\{1+o(1)\right\},

where the third equality holds due to Lemmas
S.1.1
and
S.1.4
, and the last equality above holds because of Lemma
S.1.2
, and the last second equality above holds because of Lemmas
S.1.1
,
S.1.2
and
S.1.4
,

\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii})\leq\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{ii})=\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i})=O(T)

and

0\leq\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\}/\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|\leq C\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})/\left\|\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\|=O(1).

The normality of
S_{N}
has yet to be proven. Define

\sum_{j=2}^{k}Z_{j}
where

Z_{j}=\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}\hat{\rho}_{ij},

Let

\mathcal{F}_{j}\doteq\sigma\{\epsilon_{1\cdot},\cdots,\epsilon_{j\cdot}\}
be the
\sigma
-field
generated by
\{\epsilon_{i\cdot},i\leq j\}
, where
\epsilon_{i\cdot}=(\epsilon_{i1},\cdots,\epsilon_{iT})^{\prime}
.
Because
\epsilon_{1\cdot},\cdots,\epsilon_{N\cdot}
are mutually independent under
H_{0}.

Obviously,
\mathbb{E}(Z_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}_{j-1})=0
and it follows that
\{\sum_{j=2}^{k}Z_{j},\mathcal{F}_{k};2\leq k\leq N\}
is a zero mean martingale.
According to the Martingale
central limit theorem in
Hall and Heyde 2014
, we only need to show

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right)}{\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}}\mathop{\rightarrow}\limits^{p}1,

(S.2.8)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right)\right\}=o\{\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\}.

(S.2.9)

We first focus on (
S.2.8
).
It can be shown that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}(Z_{j}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{j-1})=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\{\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\hat{\rho}_{ij}\right\}^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{j-1}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{j-1}\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{j-1}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A+B,

where

\displaystyle A=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\hat{\rho}_{ij}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right\}\,\,\mbox{and}\,\,B=\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right).

. By Lemmas
S.1.1
,

and

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\Bigg\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left({\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{var}\left(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\Bigg\}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\Bigg[\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}+\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)\{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}^{2}\right)+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj})\}}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}-\frac{\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj}]}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\Bigg]+O\left(T^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{1}+A_{2}-A_{3}-A_{4}+O\left(T^{-2}\right).

By Lemmas
S.1.1
and
S.1.2

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(A_{1}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+O\left\{\frac{1}{T\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\left\{1+o(1)\right\}

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(A_{1}\right)=

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{2\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq N}\sum_{1\leq i\leq j_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j_{1}}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j_{2}}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j_{1}}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j_{2}}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{2\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq N}\sum_{1\leq i\leq j_{1}-1}\frac{\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j_{1}}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j_{2}}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}}\right\}}}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j_{1}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j_{2}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right),

where the last second equality holds because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}}\right\}=

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})]}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+O\left\{T^{-1}\mathrm{tr}^{-2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)+(2+|\gamma_{2}|)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+O\left\{T^{-1}\mathrm{tr}^{-2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(T^{-2}).

Hence, we can conclude that

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(|A_{2}|\right)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)\{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}^{2}\right)+|\gamma_{2}|\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj})\}}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}^{2}\right)+|\gamma_{2}|\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj})}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}^{2}\right)+|\gamma_{2}|\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj})}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+O\left(T^{-1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right),

where the last two equality holds due to

Hence, we have
