by Markov inequality.
Next, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(A_{3}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{1}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{1}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+O\left(T^{-1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(A_{4}/\gamma_{2})=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj}]}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2C}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot}\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2C}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{P}_{i}\epsilon_{i\cdot})}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2C}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i})}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}_{i})}+O\left(\frac{1}{T\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right).

Because
A_{3}
and
A_{4}/\gamma_{2}
are non-negative, we have
A_{3}=o_{p}\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right)
and
A_{4}/\gamma_{2}=o_{p}\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right).

Second, we focus on
B.

\displaystyle B=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\left\{\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\left\{\frac{4}{N(N-1)}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\right\},

where we denote that
\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\doteq\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\right|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}\right).

Obviously, we have
\mathbb{E}\left(B\right)=0
due to the law of total expectation.
By Lemmas
S.1.1
and
S.1.3
, we have

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}}{\sqrt{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\{2\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j})+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{jj}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj})\}}{\sqrt{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}}\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj}]}{\sqrt{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)

for any
1\leq i_{1}<i_{2}<j\leq N.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\delta^{2}_{i_{1}i_{2}}\right)\leq

\displaystyle 5\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right\}+5\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{(2+|\gamma_{2}|)^{2}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\right)}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\mathrm{tr}^{6}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle+5\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{4\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right\}+5\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\gamma_{2}^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{jj}]}{\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{1}\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{i_{2}\cdot}\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}\right\}+O\left(T^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{5\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\right)}+\frac{5(2+|\gamma_{2}|)^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{6}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}

\displaystyle+\frac{20\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\mathbf{M}^{2}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+\frac{5C^{2}\gamma^{2}_{2}T\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{1}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i_{2}}\right)\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}+O\left(T^{-3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-3}\right),

where the last two inequality holds due to

0\leq\mathrm{tr}^{2}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\epsilon_{i_{1}}\epsilon_{i_{2}}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii}]\leq TC^{2}(\epsilon_{i_{2}}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\epsilon_{i_{2}})(\epsilon_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{j}\epsilon_{i_{1}}),

by Lemma
S.1.3
, for some constant
C\geq 0.

So, we can calculate
\mathbb{E}(B^{2}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(B^{2}\right)\leq

\displaystyle N\sum_{j=2}^{N}\frac{16}{N^{2}(N-2)^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\underset{i_{3}<i_{4}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\delta_{i_{3}i_{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16}{N(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\mathbb{E}\left(\delta^{2}_{i_{1}i_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-3}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right),

then, we obtain that
B=o_{p}\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right).

Next, we will focus on (
S.2.9
) and prove that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}\right)=o\{\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\}

due to
\mathbb{E}\left\{\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right)\right\}=\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}\right).

We divide
\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}\right)
into two parts:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{j-1}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{j-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{j-1}\sum_{i_{4}=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{2}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{3}j}\hat{\rho}_{i_{4}j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right)+\frac{12}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\underset{i_{1}\neq i_{2}}{\sum^{j-1}\sum^{j-1}}\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{2}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}^{2}_{i_{2}j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C+D.

By Lemma
S.1.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(\left.\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)+\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})]}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mathbb{E}\left\{\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\}\{2\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\right)+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{Q}_{ii}\circ\mathbf{Q}_{ii})\}}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\mathbb{E}\left\{\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\}-2\mathbb{E}\left\{\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{3}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)\Bigg].

Then, by Lemma
S.1.4
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)^{2}|\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left({\epsilon}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}{\epsilon}_{i\cdot}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{4}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right]

\displaystyle+8\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle+8\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\gamma_{4}\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+8\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right],

where the last inequality holds because the last four terms on the right side of the first equation above are all greater than or equal to zero, according to Lemma
S.1.3
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right)\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\frac{(3+|\gamma_{2}|)\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+\frac{3(3+|\gamma_{2}|)(2+|\gamma_{2}|)\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\right)}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{tr}^{-3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{-2}\Big\{\gamma_{4}\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle+8\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\left[(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]\Big\}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\frac{(3+|\gamma_{2}|)\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+\frac{3(3+|\gamma_{2}|)(2+|\gamma_{2}|)\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\right)}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{tr}^{-3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{-2}\Big\{C|\gamma_{4}|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle+2C|\gamma_{2}|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle+8|\gamma_{2}|(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{3/2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{3}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{1/2}

\displaystyle+4C|\gamma_{2}|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]

\displaystyle+4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]\Big\}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)\Bigg).

Because
8|\gamma_{2}|(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{3/2}(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}^{3}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{1/2}/(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}=O(1),

\displaystyle\frac{4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]}{(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\gamma_{1}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{T}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{sk}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{ss}\big(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\big)_{kk}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\gamma_{1}^{2}O(1)\sqrt{T^{2}\mathrm{tr}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)^{2}}=O(T)

and

\displaystyle 4\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\gamma_{1}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{T}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{sk}^{2}\big(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\big)_{sk}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\gamma_{1}^{2}O(1)\mathrm{tr}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1),

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right)\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{(3+|\gamma_{2}|)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+\frac{3(3+|\gamma_{2}|)(2+|\gamma_{2}|)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}}{\left(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\right)^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathrm{tr}^{-3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{-2}\Big\{(C|\gamma_{4}|+2C|\gamma_{2}|+4C|\gamma_{2}|)(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\left[{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}\right]\Big\}\Big]

\displaystyle+O\left(T^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{(3+|\gamma_{2}|)}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+\frac{3(3+|\gamma_{2}|)(2+|\gamma_{2}|)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{2}_{i}\right)}{\mathrm{tr}^{4}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\right)}+\frac{C|\gamma_{4}|+2C|\gamma_{2}|+4C|\gamma_{2}|}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\frac{2\mathrm{tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)^{2}\right\}+\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)+\gamma_{2}\mathrm{tr}\{(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{M}_{i}\mathbf{P}_{j}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\}}{\mathrm{tr}^{2}\left(\mathbf{M}_{j}\right)}

\displaystyle+O\left(T^{-1}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big\{\frac{{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}}{\mathrm{tr}^{3}(\mathbf{M}_{i})(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}}\Big\}+O\left(T^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right),

where the last equality holds because of the fact

\displaystyle\frac{2\gamma_{1}^{2}{\tau}_{T}^{\prime}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{T}\circ(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}{\tau}_{T}}{(\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\gamma_{1}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{T}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{ss}\big(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\big)_{sk}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{kk}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\gamma_{1}^{2}O(1)\sum_{s=1}^{T}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{ss}\sum_{k=1}^{T}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)_{kk}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\gamma_{1}^{2}O(1)\mathrm{tr}^{2}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}\mathbf{P}_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{1/2}}{\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\hat{\epsilon}_{j\cdot}}\Big)=O(1).

(S.2.10)

So,
C=O\left(N^{-2}T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right).
Next,
we consider
D.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{2}_{i_{1}j}\hat{\rho}^{2}_{i_{2}j}\right)\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{i_{1}j}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{i_{2}j}\right)}=O\left(T^{-2}\right).

Then,
D=O\left(N^{-1}T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right)
and
\sum_{j=2}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{j}^{4}\right)=o\{\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\}.

Then, we complete the proof.
\Box

S.2.6
Proof of Lemma
S.1.11

Recall that

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{S_{N}}=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{i}-\bar{v}_{ij}\right)v_{i}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}-\bar{v}_{ij}\right),

where
\bar{v}_{ij}=\frac{1}{N-2}\sum_{1<\tau\neq i,j<N}v_{\tau}
and
v_{\tau}=\hat{\epsilon}_{\tau}/\|\hat{\epsilon}_{\tau}\|.

We have

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{S_{N}}=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}-\bar{v}_{ij}\right)v_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{i}-\bar{v}_{ij}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}\right)^{2}-\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{i}+\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}v_{j}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F_{1}-F_{2}-F_{3}+F_{4}.

Obviously,
\mathbb{E}\left(F_{1}\right)=\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\left\{1+o(1)\right\}
and

\mathbb{E}\left(F_{i}\right)=0
for
i=2,3,4.
Then, we just need to prove that
\mathrm{var}\left(F_{1}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right)
and

\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{i}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right)

for
i=2,\cdots,4.

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(F^{2}_{1}\right)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}\right)^{4}\right\}+\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}<i_{3}}{\sum\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{2}}\right)^{2}\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}<i_{3}}{\sum\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{2}\left(v_{i_{2}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\underset{i_{1}<i_{2}<i_{3}}{\sum\sum\sum}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{2}}\right)^{2}\left(v_{i_{2}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}.

Because
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{2}}\right)^{2}\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{2}}\right)^{4}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{3}}\right)^{4}\right\}}
and
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{i_{2}}\right)^{4}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{i_{1}i_{2}}\right)=O\left(T^{-2}\right),

we have
\mathrm{var}\left(F^{2}_{1}\right)=O\left(N^{-1}T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right).

We next deal with
F_{2}.

F_{2}=\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}=\frac{2}{N(N-1)(N-2)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sum_{\tau\neq i,j}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau}.

So,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{2}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j_{1}=2}^{N}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{j_{1}-1}\sum_{\tau_{1}\neq i_{1},j_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=2}^{N}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{j_{2}-1}\sum_{\tau_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i_{1}}^{\prime}v_{j_{1}}v_{j_{1}}^{\prime}v_{\tau_{1}}v_{i_{2}}^{\prime}v_{j_{2}}v_{j_{2}}^{\prime}v_{\tau_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\overset{j-1}{\sum_{i=1}}\sum_{\tau\neq i,j}\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{\tau}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{8}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}(N-2)^{2}}\underset{j_{1}<j_{2}}{\sum^{N}\sum^{N}}\overset{j_{1}-1}{\sum_{i=1}}\sum_{\tau\neq i,j_{1},j_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{1}}v_{j_{1}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{2}}v_{j_{2}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right).

Because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{\tau}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j}v_{j}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{2}_{ij}\hat{\rho}^{2}_{j\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{ij}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\rho}^{4}_{j\tau}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right).

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{1}}v_{j_{1}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{2}}v_{j_{2}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{1}}v_{j_{1}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)^{2}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(v_{i}^{\prime}v_{j_{2}}v_{j_{2}}^{\prime}v_{\tau}\right)^{2}\right\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{-2}\right),

we have
\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{2}\right)=O\left(N^{-2}T^{-2}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right).

Similarly, we have
F_{3}=o_{p}\left(\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}\right).
Now, we consider
F_{4},

\displaystyle F_{4}=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}v_{i}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}v_{j}^{\prime}\bar{v}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sum_{\tau_{1}\neq i,j}\sum_{\tau_{2}\neq i,j}v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N(N-1)(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sum_{\tau\neq i,j}v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau}+\frac{2}{N(N-1)(N-2)^{2}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sum_{\tau_{1}\neq\tau_{2}\neq i,j}v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F_{41}+F_{42}.

So,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{41}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}(N-2)^{4}}\sum_{j=2}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sum_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\neq i,j}\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(N^{-4}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{1}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}v_{j}^{\prime}{v}_{\tau_{2}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(N^{-4}T^{-2}\right).

Similarly, we have
\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{42}\right)=O\left(N^{-4}T^{-2}\right).

Next, we can conclude that
\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{4}\right)=o\left(\sigma^{4}_{S_{N}}\right)
due to

\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{4}\right)\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left(F^{2}_{41}+F^{2}_{42}\right),

which leads to

\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{S_{N}}/{\sigma}^{2}_{S_{N}}\rightarrow 1,

in probability.
\Box

S.2.7
Proof of Lemma
S.1.12

By Theorem
2.1
and Lemma
S.1.10
, the following hold,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-4\log N+\log\log N\rightarrow G(y)\,\,\text{in distribution; }

(S.2.11)

\displaystyle S_{N}/\hat{\sigma}_{S_{N}}\rightarrow\mathcal{N}(0,1)\text{ in distribution. }

(S.2.12)

First, we will prove that if
\tilde{T}_{max}-\log N+\log\log N
and
S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}
are asymptotically independent,
then
\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\log N+\log\log N
and
S_{N}/{\sigma}_{S_{N}}
are also asymptotically
independent.
Due to (
S.1.4
), (
S.1.1
) and (
S.1.13
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\tilde{T}_{max}\rightarrow 0,

(S.2.13)

in probability.
Then, to show asymptotic independence, it is enough to show

\displaystyle\lim_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-4\log N+\log\log N\leq y\right)=\Phi(x)\cdot G(y),

(S.2.14)

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
y\in\mathbb{R},
where
\Phi(x)=(2\pi)^{-1/2}\int_{-\infty}^{x}e^{-t^{2}/2}dt
and
G(y)=\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{8\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y}{2}\right)\right\}.
Let

a_{N}\doteq 4\log N-\log\log N+y.

Due to (
S.2.11
) and (
S.2.12
), we know (
S.2.14
) is equivalent to that

\displaystyle\lim_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)=\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y)\right\},

(S.2.15)

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}
. By the assumption, we know that

\displaystyle\lim_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\tilde{T}_{max}>a_{N}\right)=\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y)\right\},

(S.2.16)

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}
. We show next that (
S.2.16
) implies (
S.2.15
). By (
S.2.13
),

\left|\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\tilde{T}_{max}\right|\rightarrow 0

in probability. Given
\epsilon\in(0,1).
Set

\Omega=\left\{\left|\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\tilde{T}_{max}\right|<\epsilon\right\}.

Then

\displaystyle\lim_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\Omega\right)=1.

(S.2.17)

Now,

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N},\Omega\right)+P\left(\Omega^{c}\right).

(S.2.18)

\operatorname{On}\Omega
, if
\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}
then

\displaystyle\tilde{T}_{max}\geq\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\left|\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-\tilde{T}_{max}\right|>a_{N}-\epsilon.

(S.2.19)

Define

\displaystyle\tilde{a}_{N}\doteq 4\log N-\log\log N+y-2\epsilon,

which makes sense for large
T
. Thus,

a_{N}-\tilde{a}_{N}>\epsilon,

and (
S.2.19
) conclude that

\tilde{T}_{max}\geq\tilde{a}_{N},

as
p
is sufficiently large. Review (
S.2.7
). We have

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\tilde{T}_{max}\geq\tilde{a}_{N}\right)+P\left(\Omega^{c}\right).

Immediately from (
S.2.16
) and (
S.2.17
) we get

\limsup_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)\leq\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y-2\epsilon)\right\},

for any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
. Inspect that the left-hand side of the above does not depend on
\epsilon
. Letting
\epsilon\downarrow 0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\limsup_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)\leq\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y)\right\},

(S.2.20)

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}
. In the following we will show the lower limit. Evidently,

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N},\Omega\right).

(S.2.21)

Set

\tilde{a}_{N}^{\prime}\doteq 4\log N-\log\log N+y+2\epsilon,

Therefore,

\tilde{a}_{N}^{\prime}>a_{N}+\epsilon.

It is straightforward to verify that

\displaystyle\left\{\tilde{T}_{max}>\tilde{a}_{N}^{\prime},\Omega\right\}\subset\left\{\tilde{T}_{max}>a_{N}+\epsilon,\Omega\right\}\subset\left\{\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N},\Omega\right\},

(S.2.22)

as
N
is sufficiently large, where the last inclusion follows from the definition of
\Omega.
By (
S.2.7
),

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\tilde{T}_{max}>\tilde{a}_{N}^{\prime},\Omega\right).

Thus, from (
S.2.16
) and (
S.2.17
) we get

\liminf_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)\geq\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y+2\epsilon)\right\},

for any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
. Sending
\epsilon\downarrow 0
we see

\liminf_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}P\left(S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}\leq x,\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}>a_{N}\right)\geq\Phi(x)\cdot\left\{1-G(y)\right\},

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}
.

We have proved that
\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-4\log N+\log\log N
and
S_{N}/\sigma_{S_{N}}
are asymptotically independent.
Similarly, it is easy to prove that
\frac{\mathrm{tr}^{2}(\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}})}{\|\tilde{\mathbf{\Sigma}}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{F}}}L_{N}-4\log N+\log N
and
S_{N}/\hat{\sigma}_{S_{N}}
are also asymptotically
independent. Consequently, we complete the proof.

\Box

S.2.8
Proof of Lemma
S.1.13

For
I_{l}
appeared in
H(N,k),
write
I_{l}=\left(i_{l},j_{l}\right)
for
l=1,\cdots,k.
Now we classify the indices
I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{N}
in the definition of
H(N,k)
into three cases. Let
\Gamma_{N,1}
be the set of indices
\left(I_{1},\cdots,I_{k}\right)
such that no two of the
2k
indices
\left\{i_{l},j_{l};l=1,\cdots,k\right\}
are identical. Let
\Gamma_{N,2}
be the set of indices
\left(I_{1},\cdots,I_{k}\right)
such that either
i_{1}=

\cdots=i_{k}
or
j_{1}=\cdots=j_{k}.
Let
\Gamma_{N,3}
be the set of indices
I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in

\Lambda_{N}
excluding
\Gamma_{N,1}\cup\Gamma_{N,2}.
In the following we will estimate

D_{j}:=\sum_{I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Gamma_{N,j}}\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)

for
j=1,2,3
one by one. We will see
D_{1}
contributes essentially the sum in the expression of
H(N,k)
by an easy argument; the term
D_{2}
is negligible
and its computation is trivial; the term
D_{3}
is also negligible but its estimate is most involved.

Step 1: the estimate of
D_{1}
. Recall
B_{I}=\left\{\left|\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right|\geq l_{N}\right\}
if
I=(i,j)\in

\Lambda_{N}.
By the definition of
\Gamma_{N,1},
we know that
B_{I_{1}},B_{I_{2}}\cdots,B_{I_{k}}
are independent. For large
N
,

\max_{I\in\Lambda_{N}}\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I}\right)=\mathbb{P}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\epsilon_{j\cdot}\right|\geq l_{N}\right\}\leq\frac{C}{N^{2}}

where the inequality holds due to (
S.1.1
). Then,

\displaystyle D_{1}\leq\frac{C}{N^{2k}}\cdot\binom{\frac{N(N-1)}{2}}{k}\leq\frac{C}{k!}

(S.2.23)

Step
2: the estimate of
D_{2}
. Evidently, the size of
\Gamma_{N,2}
is no more than
\binom{N}{1}\cdot\binom{N}{k}\cdot 2\leq 2N^{k+1}.

Recall that
z_{i}
is the
i
-th row vector of matrix
\mathbf{Z}.

Similarly, there exists some
C_{3}>0
such that for fixed
k>0
and
a_{l}\in\{-1,1\},

1\leq l\leq k,

that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{{l}}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{{l}}\Big)}{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}>k(1+\varepsilon_{3})\right)\leq 2N^{-3k},

where
\varepsilon_{3}=C_{3}\sqrt{\log N/\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}.

Again, by Cramér type moderate deviation results in
Statulevičius 1966
and Theorem 1.1 in
Rudelson and Vershynin 2013
, for
j_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq\cdots\neq j_{k},

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\epsilon_{j_{1}\cdot}|>l_{N},\cdots,|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\epsilon_{j_{k}\cdot}|>l_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\Big|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}\epsilon_{j_{l}\cdot}\Big)\Big|>k\cdot l_{N}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\Big|z_{i1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)\Big|>k\cdot\sqrt{a_{N}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\Big|z_{i1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)\Big|}{\sqrt{\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)}}>\frac{\sqrt{ka_{N}}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon_{3}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)>k(1+\varepsilon_{3})\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\frac{\Big|z_{i1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)\Big|}{\sqrt{\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\Big)}}>\frac{\sqrt{ka_{N}}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon_{3}}}|z_{j_{1}},\cdots,z_{j_{k}}\bigg\}\Bigg]+2^{k+1}N^{-3k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\left\{1+o(1)\right\}\frac{2}{\sqrt{ka_{N}2\pi}}\exp\left(-ka_{N}/2\right)+2^{k+1}N^{-3k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2^{k+1}(2\pi)^{-1/2}}{\sqrt{4k\log N}}\exp\left[-2k\log N+k\log\log N/2-ky/2\right]+2^{k+1}N^{-3k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{k+1}\left(\log N\right)^{k/2-1/2}N^{-2k}\exp(-ky/2)+2^{k+1}N^{-3k},

(S.2.24)

where
ka_{N}=4k\log N-k\log\log N+ky.
So, for
k\geq 2,
we have

\displaystyle D_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2N^{k+1}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=2N^{k+1}\cdot 2^{k+1}\left(\log N\right)^{k/2-1/2}N^{-2k}\exp(-ky/2)\rightarrow 0.

(S.2.25)

Step 3 : the estimate of
D_{3}
. Fix a tuple
\left(I_{1},I_{2},\cdots,I_{l}\right)\in\Gamma_{N,3}
. By the ordering imposed on
\Lambda_{N},
we see that
i_{1}\leq i_{2}\leq\cdots\leq i_{k}.
Let’s assume that

i_{1}=i_{2}=\cdots=i_{n_{1}},

i_{n_{1}+1}=i_{n_{1}+2}=\cdots=i_{n_{1}+n_{2}},

\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots

i_{n_{1}+\cdots+n_{r-1}+1}=i_{n_{1}+\cdots+n_{r-1}+2}=\cdots=i_{n_{1}+\cdots+n_{r}},

where
n_{1}+\cdots+n_{r}=k.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{1}
be the set of random vectors
\left\{\epsilon_{j_{1}\cdot},\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot},\epsilon_{j_{l}\cdot};2\leq l\leq k\right\}
.

Similar to (
S.2.8
),
for sufficiently large constant
C>0,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left\{\min_{1\leq l\leq n_{1}}|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}\epsilon_{j_{l}\cdot}|\geq l_{N}\mid\mathcal{F}_{1}\right\}\cdot\prod_{l=n_{1}+1}^{k}I\left(B_{I_{l}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\Big|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}\epsilon_{j_{l}\cdot})\Big|\geq l_{N}\mid\mathcal{F}_{1}\right\}\cdot\prod_{l=n_{1}+1}^{k}I\left(B_{I_{l}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{a_{l}\in\{-1,1\}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\Big|\epsilon_{i_{1}\cdot}^{\prime}(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}\epsilon_{j_{l}\cdot})\Big|\geq l_{N}\mid\mathcal{F}_{1}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\cdot I\Big\{\frac{\big(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\big(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\big)}{n_{1}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}>1+C\sqrt{\frac{\log N}{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}}\Big\}\cdot\prod_{l=n_{1}+1}^{k}I\left(B_{I_{l}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[I\Big\{\frac{\big(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\big)^{\prime}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2}\big(\sum_{l=1}^{n_{1}}a_{l}z_{j_{l}}\big)}{n_{1}\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}>1+C\sqrt{\frac{\log N}{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{\Sigma}^{2})}}\Big\}\cdot\prod_{l=n_{1}+1}^{k}I\left(B_{I_{l}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(N^{-2n_{1}})\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{l=n_{1}+1}^{k}I\left(B_{I_{l}}\right)\right]+2N^{-3k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(N^{-2n_{1}})\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{n_{1}+1}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)+2N^{-3k}.

In the same way, we finally obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\prod_{l=1}^{n_{1}}B_{I_{l}}\Big)\cdots\mathbb{P}\Big(\prod_{l=n_{r-1}+1}^{n_{r}}B_{I_{l}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{N^{2n_{1}+\cdots+2n_{r}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{N^{2k}},

for some constant
C
and sufficiently large
N
.
Recall
I_{l}=\left(i_{l},j_{l}\right)
for each
1\leq l\leq k.
In view of the definition of
\Gamma_{N,3},
there are at least two of the
2k
indices from
\left\{\left(i_{l},j_{l}\right);1\leq l\leq k\right\}
are identical for any
\left(I_{1},\cdots,I_{k}\right)\in\Gamma_{N,3}.
Let
\kappa=\left|\left\{i_{l},j_{l};1\leq l\leq k\right\}\right|
for such
\left(I_{1},I_{2},\cdots,I_{k}\right).

Easily,
k+1\leq\kappa\leq 2k-1.
To see how many such
\left(I_{1},\cdots,I_{k}\right)
with
\mid\left\{i_{l},j_{l};1\leq\right.

l\leq k\}\mid=\kappa,
first pick
\kappa
many indices from
\{1,2,\cdots,N\},
which has the total number of ways
\binom{N}{\kappa}\leq N^{\kappa},
then use the
\kappa
many indices to make a
\left(I_{1},\cdots,I_{k}\right)\in\Gamma_{N,3}.
The total number of ways to do so is no more than
\kappa^{2k}
. Therefore,

\left|\Gamma_{N,3}\right|\leq\sum_{\kappa=k+1}^{2k-1}N^{\kappa}\cdot\kappa^{2k}\leq(2k)^{2k}\cdot N^{2k-1}.

Similar to the (
S.2.8
), for fixed
k
,
we can have
\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)=O(N^{-2k})
and

\displaystyle D_{3}\leq

\displaystyle(2k)^{2k}\cdot 2^{k}\left(\log N\right)^{k/2-1/2}N^{-2k}\exp(-ky/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2k)^{2k}\cdot 2^{k}\exp(-ky/2)\frac{\left(\log N\right)^{k/2-1/2}}{N}\rightarrow 0

as
N
is sufficiently large.
\Box

S.2.9
Proof of Lemma
S.1.14

For
I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{N},
write
I_{l}=\left(i_{l},j_{l}\right)
for
l=1,2,\cdots,k.
Set

\Lambda_{N,k}=\left\{\left(i_{l},j\right);i_{l}<j\leq N,1\leq l\leq k\right\}\bigcup\left\{\left(i,j_{l}\right);1\leq i<j_{l},1\leq l\leq k\right\}

for
k\geq 1.
It is easy to check that
\left|\Lambda_{N,k}\right|=\sum_{l=1}^{k}\left(N-i_{l}+j_{l}-2\right).
since
i_{l}<j_{l}
for each
l,
we see that

k(N-1)\leq\left|\Lambda_{N,k}\right|\leq\sum_{l=1}^{k}\left(N+j_{l}\right)\leq 2kN.

Recall

A_{N}=\left\{S_{N}/\sqrt{\sigma^{2}_{S_{N}}}\leq x\right\}\quad x\in\mathbb{R},

for
N\geq 3
and

S_{N}^{k}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}\sum_{(i,j)\in\Lambda_{N,k}}\hat{\rho}_{ij},

for
N\geq k\geq 1
.
Observe that
B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}
is an event generated by random vectors
\left\{\hat{\rho}_{ij};(i,j)\in\Lambda_{N,k}\right\}.
A crucial observation is that
S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}
is independent of
B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}.
It is easy to see that

\displaystyle S_{N}^{k}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}{\sum\sum}_{\left\{\left(i_{l},j\right);i_{l}<j\leq N,1\leq l\leq k\right\}}\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}{\sum\sum}_{\left\{\left(i,j_{l}\right);1\leq i<j_{l},1\leq l\leq k\right\}}\hat{\rho}_{ij_{l}}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\frac{2}{N(N-1)}}\sum_{s=1}^{k}\sum_{t=1}^{k}\hat{\rho}_{i_{s}j_{t}}

\displaystyle\doteq

\displaystyle D_{N,1}+D_{N,2}-D_{N,3}.

Fix
\epsilon\in(0,1),
for even
\tau
and
\tau>2
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|D_{N,1}\right|^{\tau}\leq k^{\tau-1}\frac{2^{\tau/2}}{N^{\tau/2}(N-1)^{\tau/2}}\sum_{l=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right|^{\tau}\right),

where

\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right|\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot}\right\}\right].

By Lemma 2 in
Feng et al. 2022
, there exists a constant
K_{\tau}>0
depending on
\tau
only such that

\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right|\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot}\right\}\leq K_{\tau}(N-i_{l})^{\tau/2-1}\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right|\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot}\right\}.

So, we have

\mathbb{E}\left|D_{N,1}\right|^{\tau}\leq k^{\tau-1}\frac{2^{\tau/2}}{N^{\tau/2}(N-1)^{\tau/2}}\sum_{l=1}^{k}K_{\tau}(N-i_{l})^{\tau/2-1}\sum_{j=i_{l}+1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right|\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot}\right\}\right].

By Khintchine’s inequality (Exercise 2.6.5 in
Vershynin 2018
) and Lemma
S.1.1
, it’s easy to obtain that

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\left.\left(\hat{\rho}_{i_{l}j}\right)^{\tau}\right|\epsilon_{i_{l}\cdot}\right\}\right]\leq(\tau/2)^{\tau/4}O\left(T^{-\tau/2}\right).

Hence, together with
\sigma_{S_{N}}=O\left(T^{-1/2}\right),
we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|D_{N,1}\right|>\sigma_{S_{N}}\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left|D_{N,1}\right|^{\tau}}{\sigma^{\tau}_{S_{N}}\epsilon^{\tau}}

\displaystyle=\frac{K_{\tau}k^{\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}2^{\tau/4}}{\epsilon^{\tau}}\cdot O\left(N^{-\tau/2}\right),

where the last equality holds due to Assumption
2
.
Similarly,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|D_{N,2}\right|>\sigma_{S_{N}}\epsilon\right)\leq\frac{k^{\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}2^{\tau/4}}{\epsilon^{\tau}}\cdot O\left(N^{-\tau/2}\right).

Lastly, for even
\tau
and
\tau>2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left|D_{N,3}\right|^{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{\tau/2}}{N^{\tau/2}(N-1)^{\tau/2}}\cdot k^{2(\tau-1)}\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{k}\sum_{t=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\hat{\rho}_{i_{s}j_{t}}\right|^{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{\tau/2}}{N^{\tau/2}(N-1)^{\tau/2}}\cdot k^{2\tau}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\hat{\rho}_{i_{s}j_{t}}\right|^{\tau}\right).

So, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{|S_{N}^{k}|}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\geq\epsilon\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}},

for large
N
, where
C^{\prime}
is a constant depending on
\epsilon
but free of
N.

Fix
I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{N}.
By the definition of
A_{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}},\frac{\left|S_{N}^{k}\right|}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}<\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x+\epsilon,B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x+\epsilon\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}},

by the independence between
S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}
and
B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}.
Now

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x+\epsilon\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x+\epsilon,\frac{\left|S_{N}^{k}\right|}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}<\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x+2\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x+2\epsilon)\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}.

Combing the two inequalities to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x+2\epsilon)\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)+2C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}.

(S.2.26)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-\epsilon,B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-\epsilon,B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}},\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\left|S_{N}^{k}\right|}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}<\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x,B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}.

In other words, by independence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-\epsilon\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)-C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-2\epsilon\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-2\epsilon,\frac{\left|S_{N}^{k}\right|}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}<\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}-S_{N}^{k}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-\epsilon\right\}+C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}.

(S.2.27)

The above two strings of inequalities imply

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{S_{N}}{\sigma_{S_{N}}}\leq x-2\epsilon\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)-2C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}},

which joining with (
S.2.9
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right\}-\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta_{N,\epsilon}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)+4C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}},

where

\Delta_{N,\epsilon}\doteq\left|\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)\right\}-\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x+2\epsilon)\right\}\right|+\left|\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)\right\}-\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x-2\epsilon)\right\}\right|.

In particular,

\displaystyle\Delta_{N,\epsilon}\rightarrow|\Phi(x+2\epsilon)-\Phi(x)|+|\Phi(x-2\epsilon)-\Phi(x)|,

(S.2.28)

as
\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty
by Theorem
2.1
. As a consequence,

\displaystyle\zeta(N,k)\doteq

\displaystyle\sum_{I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{N}}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(A_{N}(x)B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\mathbb{P}\left\{A_{N}(x)\right\}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{N}}\left\{\Delta_{N,\epsilon}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)+4C^{\prime}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta_{N,\epsilon}\cdot H(N,k)+\left(4C^{\prime}\right)\cdot\binom{\frac{1}{2}N(N-1)}{k}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}},

where

H(N,k)=\sum_{I_{1}<I_{2}<\cdots<I_{k}\in\Lambda_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left(B_{I_{1}}B_{I_{2}}\cdots B_{I_{k}}\right)

as defined in Lemmas
S.1.13
and
S.1.14
, we know
\lim\sup_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}H(N,k)\leq C/k!
, where
C
is a universal constant. Picking
\tau=6k,
and using the trivial fact
\binom{r}{i}\leq r^{i}
for any integers
1\leq i\leq r,
we have that

\dbinom{\frac{1}{2}N(N-1)}{k}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}\leq N^{2k}\cdot\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{\tau/2}}\leq\frac{k^{2\tau}\tau^{\tau/4}}{N^{k}}\rightarrow 0.

Hence, from (
S.2.28
)

\displaystyle\limsup_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}\zeta(N,k)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{k!}\cdot\limsup_{\min(N,T)\rightarrow\infty}\Delta_{N,\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\frac{C}{k!}\cdot\left\{|\Phi(x+2\epsilon)-\Phi(x)|+|\Phi(x-2\epsilon)-\Phi(x)|\right\},

for any
\epsilon>0.
The desired result follows by sending
\epsilon\downarrow 0
.

\Box
